   },\n411:                     else => break,\n412:                 }\n413:             }\n414:         }\n415:     }\n416: }\n417: \n418: impl hyper::server::accept::Accept for HttpsAcceptor {\n419:   [Clippy Documentation](https://doc.rust-lang.org/nightly/clippy)\n\n# Crate toml [\\[−\\]](javascript:void\\(0\\) \"collapse all docs\") [\\[src\\]](../src/toml/.cargo/registry/src/github.com-1ecc6299db9ec823/toml-0.2.0/src/lib.rs.html#1-492 \"goto source code\")\n\nA TOML-parsing library\n\nThis library is an implementation in Rust of a parser for TOML configuration files [1](https://github.com/mojombo/toml). It is focused around high quality errors including specific spans and detailed error messages when things go wrong.\n\nThis implementation currently passes the language agnostic [test suite](https://github.com/BurntSushi/toml-test).\n\n# [Example](#example)\n\nlet toml \\= r#\"\n    \\[test\\]\n    foo = \"bar\"\n\"#;\n\nlet value \\= toml::Parser::new(toml).parse().unwrap();\nprintln!(\"{:?}\", value);\n\n# [Conversions](#conversions)\n\nThis library also supports using the standard `Encodable` and `Decodable` traits with TOML values. This library provides the following conversion capabilities:\n\n-   `String` => `toml::Value` - via `Parser`\n-   `toml::Value` => `String` - via `Display`\n-   `toml::Value` => rust object - via `Decoder`\n-   rust object => `toml::Value` - via `Encoder`\n\nConvenience functions for performing multiple conversions at a time are also provided.\n\n## [Structs](#structs)\n\n[DecodeError](struct.DecodeError.html \"toml::DecodeError\")\n\nDescription for errors which can occur while decoding a type.\n\n[Decoder](struct.Decoder.html values.\n\n[EncoderState](struct.EncoderState.html \"toml::EncoderState\")\n\nInternal state of the encoder when encoding transitions\n\n[Parser](struct.Parser.html \"toml::Parser\")\n\nParser for
